Model chấmRunpod Qwen3 8B Q4 · qwen3-8b-q4

Kết quả chấm bài

Substantiated behaviors include: sample3.exe with hash 356029C1D0D833FB6582401015042222DE2BC5C6DD9612AB952938E2CD7EF383, GUI subsystem execution (PID 4834), low-level keyboard hook (SetWindowsHookEx), ADS append to win.ini:secret_log, persistence via HKCU Run key, no network activity. Unique defects: 1. Missing file drop (ADS write to win.ini:secret_log); 2. Incorrect PID (8483 instead of 4834); 3. Unsupported claim of svchost injection; 4. Contradiction in persistence (claims no changes vs. Run key creation); 5. Placeholder filename (kl_ads.exe instead of sample3.exe).

| Lưu ý môi trường | Ổ đĩa NTFS (bắt buộc để quan sát Alternate Data Stream); chỉ gõ dữ liệu giả. |
